{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"gen-1783830714-5jWIWh5yGu6Jog2DpaCE","type":"message","role":"assistant","container":null,"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_B9hYxQa8Dzw8ZrWIZ7vMJR3r","caller":{"type":"direct"},"name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/build_ship_block_plan.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Generate ship block capacity plan.\n\nReads /root/ship_demand.csv (weeks as columns, demand as row 2), transposes it\ninto a per-week demand dictionary, applies the deterministic planning policy,\nand writes:\n  - /root/ship_block_plan.xlsx (sheet: Plan)\n  - /root/ship_block_summary.txt\n\nAlso performs the verification steps requested by the task.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport csv\nimport re\nfrom decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom openpyxl import Workbook, load_workbook\n\n\nCSV_PATH = Path(\"/root/ship_demand.csv\")\nXLSX_PATH = Path(\"/root/ship_block_plan.xlsx\")\nSUMMARY_PATH = Path(\"/root/ship_block_summary.txt\")\n\nQ2 = Decimal(\"0.01\")\n\n\ndef q2(x: Decimal) -> Decimal:\n    # Avoid -0.00\n    y = x.quantize(Q2, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)\n    if y == Decimal(\"-0.00\"):\n        return Decimal(\"0.00\")\n    return y\n\n\ndef read_demand_by_week(csv_path: Path) -> dict[int, Decimal]:\n    with csv_path.open(newline=\"\") as f:\n        rows = [row for row in csv.reader(f) if any(cell.strip() for cell in row)]\n\n    if len(rows) < 2:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Expected at least 2 non-empty rows in {csv_path}\")\n\n    header = rows[0]\n    demand_row = rows[1]\n\n    if not header or header[0].strip() != \"Week\":\n        raise ValueError(\"CSV row 1 col 1 must be 'Week'\")\n    if not demand_row or demand_row[0].strip() != \"Demand\":\n        raise ValueError(\"CSV row 2 col 1 must be 'Demand'\")\n\n    if len(header) != len(demand_row):\n        raise ValueError(\"Header and demand row must have the same number of columns\")\n\n    demand: dict[int, Decimal] = {}\n    for i in range(1, len(header)):\n        week_str = header[i].strip()\n        val_str = demand_row[i].strip()\n        if not week_str:\n            continue\n        if not val_str:\n            continue\n        week = int(week_str)\n        demand[week] = q2(Decimal(val_str))\n\n    # Ensure weeks 5..53 exist\n    missing = [w for w in range(5, 54) if w not in demand]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Missing demand values for weeks: {missing}\")\n\n    return demand\n\n\ndef build_plan(demand: dict[int, Decimal]) -> list[dict[str, object]]:\n    plan_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []\n\n    prior_end_of_week_backlog: Decimal | None = None\n\n    # Summary trackers\n    any_6 = False\n    first_5_any: int | None = None\n    first_stepdown_6to5: int | None = None\n    first_4: int | None = None\n    previous_days: int | None = None\n\n    for w in range(5, 54):\n        scheduled_demand = demand[w]\n\n        if w == 5:\n            calc_start = q2(Decimal(\"1014.51\") - scheduled_demand)\n        else:\n            if prior_end_of_week_backlog is None:\n                raise RuntimeError(\"prior_end_of_week_backlog not set\")\n            calc_start = q2(prior_end_of_week_backlog)\n\n        start_past_due = q2(max(Decimal(\"0\"), calc_start))\n\n        # Choose Days Worked\n        if start_past_due > Decimal(\"0.01\"):\n            # Try 5 first, then 6\n            if calc_start + scheduled_demand - Decimal(28 * 5) <= 0:\n                days = 5\n            elif calc_start + scheduled_demand - Decimal(28 * 6) <= 0:\n                days = 6\n            else:\n                days = 6\n        else:\n            if scheduled_demand <= Decimal(\"112\"):\n                days = 4\n            else:\n                days = 5\n\n        capacity = Decimal(28 * days)\n        end_backlog = q2(calc_start + scheduled_demand - capacity)\n        overtime = Decimal(10 * max(0, days - 4))\n\n        prior_end_of_week_backlog = end_backlog\n\n        plan_rows.append(\n            {\n                \"Week\": w,\n                \"Days Worked\": days,\n                \"Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)\": float(scheduled_demand),\n                \"Weekly Capacity (Std Hrs)\": float(capacity),\n                \"Start of Week Past Due (Std Hrs)\": float(start_past_due),\n                \"End of Week Backlog/Buffer (Std Hrs)\": float(end_backlog),\n                \"Overtime Hours\": float(overtime),\n            }\n        )\n\n        # Track summary weeks\n        if days == 6:\n            any_6 = True\n        if days == 5 and first_5_any is None:\n            first_5_any = w\n        if previous_days == 6 and days == 5 and first_stepdown_6to5 is None:\n            first_stepdown_6to5 = w\n        if days == 4 and first_4 is None:\n            first_4 = w\n\n        previous_days = days\n\n    # Compute first_week_5_days per instructions\n    if any_6:\n        first_week_5_days = first_stepdown_6to5 if first_stepdown_6to5 is not None else first_5_any\n    else:\n        first_week_5_days = first_5_any\n\n    # Keep it short, manager-facing, and always mention both values.\n    summary = (\n        f\"Plan uses 6-day weeks as needed to catch up, then steps down to 5 days in week {first_5_str} \"\n        f\"and to 4 days in week {first_4_str}.\"\n    )\n\n    # Hard guardrails (≤60 words, ≤3 sentences)\n    words = summary.split()\n    if len(words) > 60:\n        summary = \" \".join(words[:60])\n        if not summary.endswith(\".\"):\n            summary += \".\"\n\n    # Ensure ≤3 sentences by stripping extra sentence terminators if any\n    # (Our template is one sentence, so this is mostly defensive.)\n    sentence_count = len([s for s in re.split(r\"[.!?]+\", summary) if s.strip()])\n    if sentence_count > 3:\n        # Reduce to first 3 sentences.\n        parts = re.split(r\"([.!?]+)\", summary)\n        out = []\n        sentences = 0\n        for i in range(0, len(parts), 2):\n            frag = parts[i]\n            punct = parts[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(parts) else \"\"\n            if frag.strip():\n                out.append(frag + punct)\n                if punct:\n                    sentences += 1\n            if sentences >= 3:\n                break\n        summary = \"\".join(out).strip()\n\n    return summary\n\n\ndef write_summary(plan_rows: list[dict[str, object]], summary_path: Path) -> None:\n    first_5 = plan_rows[0].get(\"_first_week_5_days\")\n    first_4 = plan_rows[0].get(\"_first_week_4_days\")\n\n    first_5_str = \"N/A\" if first_5 is None else str(first_5)\n    first_4_str = \"N/A\" if first_4 is None else str(first_4)\n\n    summary = make_manager_summary(\n        None if first_5_str == \"N/A\" else int(first_5_str),\n        None if first_4_str == \"N/A\" else int(first_4_str),\n    )\n\n    content = (\n        f\"First_Week_5_Days: {first_5_str}\\n\"\n        f\"First_Week_4_Days: {first_4_str}\\n\"\n        f\"Summary: {summary}\\n\"\n    )\n    summary_path.write_text(content)\n\n\ndef verify_outputs(xlsx_path: Path, summary_path: Path) -> None:\n    wb = load_workbook(xlsx_path)\n    if \"Plan\" not in wb.sheetnames:\n        raise AssertionError(\"Expected sheet named 'Plan'\")\n    ws = wb[\"Plan\"]\n\n    expected_headers = [\n        \"Week\",\n        \"Days Worked\",\n        \"Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Weekly Capacity (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Start of Week Past Due (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"End of Week Backlog/Buffer (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Overtime Hours\",\n    ]\n\n    headers = [ws.cell(row=1, column=c).value for c in range(1, 8)]\n    if headers != expected_headers:\n        raise AssertionError(f\"Header mismatch. Avoid -0.00\n    y = x.quantize(Q2, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)\n    if y == Decimal(\"-0.00\"):\n        return Decimal(\"0.00\")\n    return y\n\n\ndef read_demand_by_week(csv_path: Path) -> dict[int, Decimal]:\n    with csv_path.open(newline=\"\") as f:\n        rows = [row for row in csv.reader(f) if any(cell.strip() for cell in row)]\n\n    if len(rows) < 2:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Expected at least 2 non-empty rows in {csv_path}\")\n\n    header = rows[0]\n    demand_row = rows[1]\n\n    if not header or header[0].strip() != \"Week\":\n        raise ValueError(\"CSV row 1 col 1 must be 'Week'\")\n    if not demand_row or demand_row[0].strip() != \"Demand\":\n        raise ValueError(\"CSV row 2 col 1 must be 'Demand'\")\n\n    if len(header) != len(demand_row):\n        raise ValueError(\"Header and demand row must have the same number of columns\")\n\n    demand: dict[int, Decimal] = {}\n    for i in range(1, len(header)):\n        week_str = header[i].strip()\n        val_str = demand_row[i].strip()\n        if not week_str:\n            continue\n        if not val_str:\n            continue\n        week = int(week_str)\n        demand[week] = q2(Decimal(val_str))\n\n    # Ensure weeks 5..53 exist\n    missing = [w for w in range(5, 54) if w not in demand]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Missing demand values for weeks: {missing}\")\n\n    return demand\n\n\ndef build_plan(demand: dict[int, Decimal]) -> list[dict[str, object]]:\n    plan_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []\n\n    prior_end_of_week_backlog: Decimal | None = None\n\n    # Summary trackers\n    any_6 = False\n    first_5_any: int | None = None\n    first_stepdown_6to5: int | None = None\n    first_4: int | None = None\n    previous_days: int | None = None\n\n    for w in range(5, 54):\n        scheduled_demand = demand[w]\n\n        if w == 5:\n            calc_start = q2(Decimal(\"1014.51\") - scheduled_demand)\n        else:\n            if prior_end_of_week_backlog is None:\n                raise RuntimeError(\"prior_end_of_week_backlog not set\")\n            calc_start = q2(prior_end_of_week_backlog)\n\n        start_past_due = q2(max(Decimal(\"0\"), calc_start))\n\n        # Choose Days Worked\n        if start_past_due > Decimal(\"0.01\"):\n            # Try 5 first, then 6\n            if calc_start + scheduled_demand - Decimal(28 * 5) <= 0:\n                days = 5\n            elif calc_start + scheduled_demand - Decimal(28 * 6) <= 0:\n                days = 6\n            else:\n                days = 6\n        else:\n            if scheduled_demand <= Decimal(\"112\"):\n                days = 4\n            else:\n                days = 5\n\n        capacity = Decimal(28 * days)\n        end_backlog = q2(calc_start + scheduled_demand - capacity)\n        overtime = Decimal(10 * max(0, days - 4))\n\n        prior_end_of_week_backlog = end_backlog\n\n        plan_rows.append(\n            {\n                \"Week\": w,\n                \"Days Worked\": days,\n                \"Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)\": float(scheduled_demand),\n                \"Weekly Capacity (Std Hrs)\": float(capacity),\n                \"Start of Week Past Due (Std Hrs)\": float(start_past_due),\n                \"End of Week Backlog/Buffer (Std Hrs)\": float(end_backlog),\n                \"Overtime Hours\": float(overtime),\n            }\n        )\n\n        # Track summary weeks\n        if days == 6:\n            any_6 = True\n        if days == 5 and first_5_any is None:\n            first_5_any = w\n        if previous_days == 6 and days == 5 and first_stepdown_6to5 is None:\n            first_stepdown_6to5 = w\n        if days == 4 and first_4 is None:\n            first_4 = w\n\n        previous_days = days\n\n    # Compute first_week_5_days per instructions\n    if any_6:\n        first_week_5_days = first_stepdown_6to5 if first_stepdown_6to5 is not None else first_5_any\n    else:\n        first_week_5_days = first_5_any\n\n    # Attach to the plan (metadata)\n    for meta_key, meta_val in (\n        (\"_first_week_5_days\", first_week_5_days),\n        (\"_first_week_4_days\", first_4),\n    ):\n        # stash on first row; avoids changing row schema\n        plan_rows[0][meta_key] = meta_val\n\n    return plan_rows\n\n\ndef write_excel(plan_rows: list[dict[str, object]], xlsx_path: Path) -> None:\n    headers = [\n        \"Week\",\n        \"Days Worked\",\n        \"Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Weekly Capacity (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Start of Week Past Due (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"End of Week Backlog/Buffer (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Overtime Hours\",\n    ]\n\n    wb = Workbook()\n    ws = wb.active\n    ws.title = \"Plan\"\n\n    ws.append(headers)\n    for row in plan_rows:\n        ws.append([row[h] for h in headers])\n\n    wb.save(xlsx_path)\n\n\ndef make_manager_summary(first_5: int | None, first_4: int | None) -> str:\n    first_5_str = \"N/A\" if first_5 is None else str(first_5)\n    first_4_str = \"N/A\" if first_4 is None else str(first_4)\n\n    # Keep it short, manager-facing, and always mention both values.\n    summary = (\n        f\"Plan uses 6-day weeks as needed to catch up, then steps down to 5 days in week {first_5_str} \"\n        f\"and to 4 days in week {first_4_str}.\"\n    )\n\n    # Hard guardrails (≤60 words, ≤3 sentences)\n    words = summary.split()\n    if len(words) > 60:\n        summary = \" \".join(words[:60])\n        if not summary.endswith(\".\"):\n            summary += \".\"\n\n    # Reduce to first 3 sentences.\n        parts = re.split(r\"([.!?]+)\", summary)\n        out = []\n        sentences = 0\n        for i in range(0, len(parts), 2):\n            frag = parts[i]\n            punct = parts[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(parts) else \"\"\n            if frag.strip():\n                out.append(frag + punct)\n                if punct:\n                    sentences += 1\n            if sentences >= 3:\n                break\n        summary = \"\".join(out).strip()\n\n    return summary\n\n\ndef write_summary(plan_rows: list[dict[str, object]], summary_path: Path) -> None:\n    first_5 = plan_rows[0].get(\"_first_week_5_days\")\n    first_4 = plan_rows[0].get(\"_first_week_4_days\")\n\n    first_5_str = \"N/A\" if first_5 is None else str(first_5)\n    first_4_str = \"N/A\" if first_4 is None else str(first_4)\n\n    summary = make_manager_summary(\n        None if first_5_str == \"N/A\" else int(first_5_str),\n        None if first_4_str == \"N/A\" else int(first_4_str),\n    )\n\n    content = (\n        f\"First_Week_5_Days: {first_5_str}\\n\"\n        f\"First_Week_4_Days: {first_4_str}\\n\"\n        f\"Summary: {summary}\\n\"\n    )\n    summary_path.write_text(content)\n\n\ndef verify_outputs(xlsx_path: Path, summary_path: Path) -> None:\n    wb = load_workbook(xlsx_path)\n    if \"Plan\" not in wb.sheetnames:\n        raise AssertionError(\"Expected sheet named 'Plan'\")\n    ws = wb[\"Plan\"]\n\n    expected_headers = [\n        \"Week\",\n        \"Days Worked\",\n        \"Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Weekly Capacity (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Start of Week Past Due (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"End of Week Backlog/Buffer (Std Hrs)\",\n        \"Overtime Hours\",\n    ]\n\n    headers = [ws.cell(row=1, column=c).value for c in range(1, 8)]\n    if headers != expected_headers:\n        raise AssertionError(f\"Header mismatch.
